| Raw materials | Family (rule or manual pick) | The content vote that gives finished goods and intermediates their family; UV text and the SDS profile on the raw material's own resale sheet | The raw material does not count toward any family; its resale sheet gets no UV text and no profile lines (Section 1 is unaffected) |
| Raw materials | Substance / Mixture setting | Section 3 Type on resale sheets and single-raw formulas | Mixture |
| Raw materials | Constituent CAS numbers, chemical names and percentages | Acrylate names in Section 4 (at 0.1 % or more), the Section 10 inhibitor sentence, water (CAS 7732-18-5) for the water-based rule, and the complete-composition test for the Section 13 cure-residue sentence | Section 4 prints without names and the inhibitor sentence is omitted. Water held only in the raw material's water % field does not count as water-based, and incomplete data suppresses the cure-residue sentence. |
| Raw materials | Trade-secret description on secret acrylate constituents | Section 4 UV sentence names | Prints "Trade Secret" (translated) |
| CAS determinations | Acrylate flag (CAS Descriptions 'Acrylate' column, or Seed acrylate flags preview + Apply) | Which components count as acrylates: Section 4 names and the Section 10 inhibitor sentence | Not set: the old 14-CAS list (one more release) or the acrylate name rule decides. Unticked (manual no): never an acrylate. |
| CAS determinations | Preferred name / description of acrylate CAS numbers | Section 4 UV sentence names (outranks the constituent name; the old hard-coded labels are gone) | Constituent chemical name is used, else the CAS number |
| Regulatory lists | Prop 65 list chemical name | Section 4 UV sentence names (first in the name chain) | CAS registry / constituent name is used |
| Settings | UV Acrylate Rule Pack switch | The UV sentences in the Section 2/8 PPE fields, Sections 4, 5 and 6, Section 7 Handling, and the Section 11 note. It does not affect Section 2 Other Hazards, Section 7 Storage, Section 10, 13 or 14. | A missing row counts as on. Switching it off removes those texts from newly generated sheets only. |
| Settings | Water-Based Threshold (%) (SDS Configuration; 1-100, default 20) | Whether products on Auto (or with no active family) are water-based (the freezing lines in Sections 7 and 10) | 20 % is used |
| SDS editor (per product) | Section 1 use text; Section 2 Other Hazards; 4 Skin; 5 Specific Hazards; 6 Containment; 7 Handling/Storage; 8 PPE; 9 pH; 10 Reactivity/Stability/Conditions to Avoid/Incompatible Materials; 11 UV Acrylate Information; 13 Disposal Methods; 14 Special Precautions (per language) | Those fields on product sheets; Section 1 and the same fields on resale sheets through the resale editor | The automatic text applies. An override replaces the whole field, including the UV or family sentence. |
| Settings | Company Emergency Phone | Section 1 Emergency line on every standard, alias and resale sheet | The settings form refuses a blank value. Never saved: publishing of those sheets is blocked on every path (private-label sheets still publish); previews omit the line and warn |
| Settings | Company Name | Section 1 Company line; PDF Author | Company line omitted, PDF Author 'SDS System', preview warning. Never saved: the config value prints, with a warning |
| Settings | Company address, city, state, zip, country | Section 1 Address line | Blank parts skipped; the line is omitted if all are blank |
| Settings | Company phone, email, website | Section 1 Phone / Email / Website lines | That line omitted; a blank phone adds a preview warning |
| Settings | Company Logo | Page 1 header logo on standard sheets | No logo |
| Settings | Legal disclaimer per language (EN, ES, FR, DE) | Disclaimer text on standard sheets, and on PL sheets whose manufacturer has none for that language | Translated default disclaimer |
| Settings | Show Sections 12–15 footnote toggle; UV rule-pack switch | Footnote after Section 15; UV/EB wording in Sections 4–8 and the UV glove / eyewear sentences | Missing setting = on |
| Settings | Water-Based Threshold (%) (1–100) | Water-based 'auto' rule: Section 7 'Protect from freezing.' and Section 10 'freezing' | 20 % (seeded by migration 060) |
| Manufacturers | Name | PL Section 1 Company; PDF Author; PDF file name tag; PL download name | Refused by the form; an older blank record blocks private-label publish |
| Manufacturers | Emergency Phone | PL Section 1 Emergency line | Private-label publish refused (manual, cascade, bulk); preview warns |
| Manufacturers | Address, city, state, zip, country, phone, email, website | PL Section 1 supplier lines; PL abbreviations | That line omitted (the company's values are never used); a blank phone adds a preview warning |
| Manufacturers | Logo | PL page 1 header logo | No logo |
| Manufacturers | Disclaimer per language | PL disclaimer text; PL abbreviations | Inherits the admin or translated disclaimer for that language |
| Finished goods | Private-label item custom code / custom description, or shared alias | PL Product Identifier; footer code; PDF Title; file names | Base product code and description. An alias that no longer belongs to the product refuses publish. |
| Finished goods | Product code and description (CMS); alias customer code and description | Product Identifier; footer code; PDF Title; file names | A blank description prints the code alone |
| Finished goods | Recommended use / restrictions columns | Section 1 use text in every language (single language) | Family text for that language, then the translated sentence (columns are no longer pre-filled) |
| Finished goods | Physical state and colour (standard vocabulary) | Section 9 State, Color, Appearance (translated on ES/FR/DE); Sections 6, 8, 14; combustible-dust check | Family default physical state, then the largest raw material's state, then 'Liquid' with a preview warning; colour line omitted |
| Product families | Recommended Use and Restrictions per language (active families) | Section 1 use text (standard and PL; not resale) | Translated standard sentence; English family text is never used on other languages |
| Product families | UV/LED flag | S2 Other Hazards; UV/EB wording in S4–S8 (with the switch), UV gloves / eyewear; S7/S10 polymerization lines; S11 note; S13 cure residue; S14 UV precaution | Not printed |
| Product families | SDS profile: storage temperature min / max (°C, both or neither) | Section 7 storage range sentence; Section 10 'temperatures above X °C' condition | No storage-temperature sentence; generic Section 10 conditions |
| Product families | SDS profile: Water-based (auto / yes / no) | Section 7 'Protect from freezing.'; Section 10 'freezing' | Auto: water (CAS 7732-18-5) at or above the threshold % |
| Product families | SDS profile: Default physical state | Section 9 Physical State and everything that reads the state (Sections 6, 8, 13, 14, combustible dust) when the product has none | Largest raw material's state, then 'Liquid' |
| Product families | SDS profile: typical pH min / max (both or neither) | Section 9 pH '(typical)' | Translated 'Not determined' |
| Raw materials | Flash point, boiling point, specific gravity, VOC, solubility | Section 9 values (flash point rounded with '(calculated)'); flammability in Sections 2, 5, 7, 10, 13, 14 | No flash point = not flammable and 'Not determined'; SG 1.0 / VOC 0 with preview warnings |
| Raw materials | Odor; appearance (resale sheets) (English) | Section 9 Odor / Appearance on every language sheet | Translated 'Not determined' / state word |
| Raw materials | Constituent trade-secret box and description | Masked identity in S3, S4 UV names, S8 (table and skin-notation line), S11, S12, S13, S15 (English description in every language) | Translated 'Trade Secret'; a trade secret on the Prop 65 list blocks publishing |
| Raw materials | Combustible Dust (Not assessed / Yes / No), from vendor SDS Section 2 | Section 2 Combustible Dust line and signal word; Section 5 and 7 dust fragments (Powder/Solid products only) | Not assessed: nothing prints; a Powder/Solid product with an unassessed powder/solid raw gets a preview and readiness warning |
| CAS determinations | Preferred chemical name; TSCA override | Chemical names in S3, S8, S11–S15 on all language sheets; TSCA sentence | Raw-material constituent name |
| CAS determinations | STOT Target Organs tab: SE / RE organs and route | H370–H373 text in Section 2, Section 4/11 symptoms and the Section 11 repeated-exposure line | Standard statement without organs; organs on only some CAS behind a code: standard statement plus a preview warning |
| CAS determinations | Acrylate box (Descriptions tab; seed via the acrylate-flags preview) | Acrylate names in the Section 4 UV skin sentence; Section 10 inhibitor sentence | Not determined: legacy CAS list or the acrylate name rule |
| CAS determinations | Element flags (nitrogen, sulfur, halogen) | Section 5 combustion products sentence; Section 10 decomposition products | Carbon monoxide and carbon dioxide only |
| SDS editor (per product) | Per-language text edits (incl. State Regulations, Other Hazards, Section 9 Appendix D lines, Section 9 measured Flash Point, Section 14 incl. Special Precautions) | The edited field on that product's sheets, standard and PL | Automatic text in that language; an English edit does not apply to other languages |
| Regulatory lists | Carcinogen, SARA 313 (PBT / special concern), HAP, SNUR, Prop 65, RCRA, TSCA lists (English names); exposure-limit Notes ('Skin') | Sections 8 and 11–15 entries on every language sheet; Section 8 skin-notation line | Translated 'none' sentence or block omitted |
| Nothing to maintain | Version, effective date, labels, banners, page size, Creator, Section 13 Contaminated Packaging, Section 14 Transport in Bulk | Section 16, footer, headings, PDF properties, Sections 13 and 14 | n/a |
| Settings | Emergency Phone (company) | Section 1 Emergency line on every standard, resale and alias SDS; company phone gate | Standard, resale and alias publish are blocked on every path. SDS Update 'Republish Selected' is refused. Bulk publishes private-label items only and marks the job failed. |
| Settings | Company Name and Phone | Section 1 Company and Phone lines; supplier warnings | Never saved: the config-file value prints. Saved blank: the line is dropped. Warning only. |
| Settings | Missing Hazard Data Gate checkbox and Missing Data Threshold (%) | Missing-federal-hazard-data gate on every publish path | Rows absent: on at 1.0%. A blank or out-of-range threshold cannot be saved; an old invalid value acts as 1.0%. |
| Settings | Water-Based Threshold (%) | Water-based rule (water CAS 7732-18-5 share of the formula) for the 'Protect from freezing' sentences in Sections 7 and 10, unless the family says Yes or No | Cannot be saved blank or outside 1–100. A missing or invalid stored value acts as 20%. A change prompts 'Bump ALL'. |
| Settings | Cron blackout windows / CMS sync auto bulk publish | When bulk publish runs | No blackout; auto bulk publish on. |
| Manufacturers | Emergency Phone (each private-label manufacturer) | Section 1 Emergency line on that manufacturer's private-label SDSs; manufacturer phone gate | That manufacturer's private-label items are refused (the form will not save it blank). |
| Manufacturers | Name (each private-label manufacturer) | Section 1 Company line, PDF Author and file name of private-label SDSs; manufacturer name gate | That manufacturer's private-label items are refused (the form will not save it blank). |
| Manufacturers | Phone (each private-label manufacturer) | Section 1 Phone line on private-label SDSs | No Phone line prints; private-label preview warning only. |
| Product families | SDS profile: Default physical state (Liquid, Paste, Gel, Solid, Powder) | Physical state of member products with none of their own: Sections 6, 8, 9, 14, the flammable-liquid, transport and combustible-dust checks; preview family note | The largest raw's state is used, then 'Liquid' with a preview warning. |
| Product families | SDS profile: storage temperature min/max, Water-based (auto/yes/no), typical pH min/max | Section 7 storage range, Sections 7 and 10 freezing sentences, Section 10 high-temperature condition, Section 9 pH (typical). Not a gate. | No storage-range wording; water-based decided by the Water-Based Threshold; Section 9 pH prints 'Not determined'. A half-filled or out-of-range pair cannot be saved. |
| Raw materials | Flash Point (and the '>' flag) | wt%-weighted product flash point (printed rounded with '(calculated)'); Flammable Liquids category in Sections 2, 5, 7, 9, 10, 13, 14 | The raw is left out of the average. With no raw carrying one, the product is not flammable and Section 9 prints 'Not determined'. It never blocks; a warning appears when the raw has a flammable-liquid ingredient. |
| Raw materials | Initial Boiling Point | Flammable Liquids Category 1 vs 2; Section 14 Packing Group I; Section 9 boiling point | With a flash point below 23 °C, Category 2 / PG II is assumed and a preview warning appears. |
| Raw materials | Combustible Dust (Not assessed / Yes / No) | Section 2 Combustible Dust class (Powder/Solid products) and the dust fragments in Sections 5 and 7; combustible-dust warning | Not assessed: no classification; a Powder/Solid product shows a preview and readiness warning. Never blocks. Not assessed <-> No does not bump the raw. |
| Raw materials | Constituents (CAS and percentages) | Sections 2, 3, 11, 12, 15; missing-hazard-data gate; TSCA warning; composition warning | The material is absent from the composition. The preview and readiness page warn, Section 15 prints TSCA 'not verified' (unless only percentages are missing), and no gate blocks. |
| Raw materials | Trade-secret box on constituents | Identity masking in every section; trade-secret Prop 65 gate | Not applicable. A ticked constituent whose CAS is on the Prop 65 list blocks every publish. |
| Raw materials | Manual hazard data (hazardous, no CAS) | Section 2 classification; TRADE_SECRET entry in the missing-data gate | Blocks as 'TRADE_SECRET (x%)' at or above the threshold when no raw in the trade-secret bucket has manual hazard data saved. |
| Raw materials | H-codes / physical state that imply aerosol, gas, flammable solid, oxidizer, peroxide, etc. | Section 14 unsupported-class check | Not applicable. Such codes (or state Gas) make Section 14 'Not determined' and block until a complete override is entered. |
| Raw materials | Specific Gravity and VOC % (or '<1% VOC') | Section 9 specific gravity and VOC values | Counted as SG 1.0 / VOC 0%; preview warning only. |
| Raw materials | Review (any user save of the RM) | Bulk/cron eligibility; readiness page verdict; resale Publish button | The FG, its aliases and its private-label items are left out of bulk with no reason given. |
| CAS determinations | Competent Person Determination for constituent CAS without federal data | Section 2 classification; missing-hazard-data gate; bulk 'reviewed' rule | Publishing is blocked on every path at or above the threshold. |
| CAS determinations | STOT Target Organs tab: single/repeated exposure organs and route per CAS | H370–H373 statements with organs in Sections 2, 4 and 11; STOT warning | The generic statement prints. When only some sources of a code have organs, the generic statement prints and the preview warns. |
| CAS determinations | Acrylate flag (CAS Descriptions 'Acrylate' column, or Seed Acrylate Flags) | Section 4 acrylate names on UV sheets; preview UV formulator note 'Acrylate content detected' | Not determined: the legacy acrylate CAS list, then the name rule, decides. |
| CAS determinations | TSCA Review status (listed/exempt/not listed; NOA note for INACTIVE) | Section 15 TSCA sentence; TSCA warning | Section 15 prints 'not verified', and a warning shows on manual publish, in previews and on the readiness page. |
| Regulatory lists | TSCA inventory import | Section 15 TSCA sentence; TSCA warning | Every product prints 'not verified' and warns. |
| Regulatory lists | Federal hazard data import | Section 2 classification; missing-hazard-data gate | The CAS counts as missing data. The import does not bump timestamps, so bulk will not republish afterwards. |
| Regulatory lists | Prop 65 list | Section 15 Prop 65 block; trade-secret Prop 65 gate | No Prop 65 matches, so the gate never fires. |
| SDS editor (per product) | Section 14 overrides: UN Number, Proper Shipping Name, Hazard Class, Packing Group, Environmental Hazards, Special Precautions for User, each language | Section 14; transport gate (core four only); n.o.s. warning | Fields print the automatic value. An incomplete core set blocks publishing in that language. Special Precautions never affects the gate. |
| SDS editor (per product) | Section 9 Flash Point edit (measured value) | Sections 5 and 9 flash point, printed as typed without '(calculated)'; flash point warnings | The formula value prints, rounded with '(calculated)'. A contradicting edit is dropped with a preview warning. |
| Finished goods | Current formula | All sections | Publish fails: 'No current formula found'. |
| Finished goods | Status (active flag) | Inactive-product gate on every path; bulk and private-label eligibility | An inactive product cannot be published anywhere. |
| Finished goods | Physical State | Sections 6, 8, 9, 14; flammable-liquid, transport (Gas = unsupported) and combustible-dust checks | Falls back to the product-family default, then the highest-wt% raw material that has one; with none, 'Liquid' is used with a preview warning. |
| Raw materials | Physical State | S9 Physical State and Appearance when neither product nor family sets one; S6 containment; S8 dust sentence; S13 D002; S14 solid/liquid entry; flammability; combustible-dust test; ATE dust route; carbon black/TiO2 rule | Skipped in favour of the next-largest raw material with a state, then 'Liquid' (preview warning); counts as bound for carbon black/TiO2 |
| Raw materials | Combustible Dust (Not assessed / Yes / No) | S2 Combustible Dust class line, signal word Warning, S5 and S7 dust sentences (Powder/Solid products only) | 'Not assessed': prints nothing; a Powder/Solid product with such Powder/Solid raws gets a preview and readiness warning. Only changes to or from Yes republish sheets |
| Raw materials | Flash Point (°C) and '>' flag | S9 Flash Point (wt%-weighted average, printed rounded with '(calculated)') and the S5 'Flash point:' line; Flammable Liquids category in S2, S5, S7, S8, S10, S13 D001 and S14 Class 3/PG | Left out of the average. With none on any raw: 'Not determined' and not flammable. A raw with a flammable-liquid ingredient and no flash point raises a preview warning |
| Raw materials | Boiling Point (°C) | S9 Initial Boiling Point; Flammable Liquids Cat 1 vs 2; S14 Packing Group I | 'Not determined'. With a flash point below 23 °C, Category 2 / PG II is assumed (preview warning) |
| Raw materials | Specific Gravity | S9 Specific Gravity; VOC (lb/gal) | Counted as 1.000; preview warning names the raw material |
| Raw materials | VOC wt% / 'less than 1%' flag | S9 VOC (lb/gal), VOC (wt%), Solids (wt%) | Counted as 0% VOC; preview warning names the raw material |
| Raw materials | Solids wt%, Exempt VOC wt%, Water wt% | S9 Solids (wt%) | Estimated as 100 − VOC − exempt − water (100% solids when nothing is entered). Water wt% does not count toward the water-based rule |
| Raw materials | Solubility | S9 Solubility | Excluded from the calculation; 'Not determined' when no raw has a value. 'Negligible' counts as 3% soluble |
| Raw materials | Odor | S9 Odor (highest-weight raw only) | 'Not determined' |
| Raw materials | Appearance | S9 Appearance on resale sheets only | Lower-case physical state, e.g. 'liquid' |
| Raw materials | Constituents: CAS and % (exact or min/max) | S3 rows and bands; S8, S11, S12, S15 bands; RCRA, SARA, HAP, carcinogen and PBT matches; water CAS 7732-18-5 for the water-based rule; S13 cure-residue completeness check; missing-hazard-data gate | A constituent with no % counts as 0 and is dropped from every section; incomplete constituent data suppresses the S13 UV cure-residue sentence |
| Raw materials | Constituents: trade-secret flag, description and declared H-codes | Identity masking in S3, S8 (table and skin-notation line), S11, S12, S13 and S15; trade-secret hazards; Prop 65 trade-secret gate | A blank description prints 'Trade Secret'; an unflagged constituent is named |
| Raw materials | Substance/Mixture | S3 Type for single-ingredient and resale products | 'Mixture' |
| Raw materials | Manual HAP entries (haps_data) | S15 HAP rows and total | Only HAP-list and category matches print |
| Finished goods | Physical State | S9 Physical State; S6; S8; S13 D002; S14; flammability and combustible-dust tests | Falls back to the family's default physical state, then the largest raw material that has a state, then 'Liquid' (preview warning) |
| Finished goods | Color | S9 Color and Appearance | Color line omitted; Appearance prints the lower-case state |
| Finished goods | Transport Product Type (or a clear description) | S14 UN number and proper shipping name for Class 3 products | Keywords in the description pick the entry; defaults to the printing-ink entry (UN1210) |
| Finished goods | Substance/Mixture | S3 Type; Substance identity row | Auto rule (single-ingredient Substance, else Mixture) |
| Finished goods | Hazard override (Flammable Liquids class or H224-H227) | Replaces the flash-point class in S2, S5, S7, S8, S10, S13 and S14; replace mode also suppresses Combustible Dust | Category from the formula flash point; a preview warning shows when the override disagrees with the printed flash point |
| Product families | UV/LED flag (and membership rules) | S2 Other Hazards UV sentence; S10 Reactivity and peroxides incompatibles (repeated in S7); S10 acrylate inhibitor sentence; S13 UV cure-residue sentence; S14 UV special-precautions sentence | Not UV: standard wording. An inactive family counts as no family |
| Product families | SDS profile: Default physical state | Resolved physical state for every product in the family with no state of its own (S6, S8, S9, S13 D002, S14, flammability, combustible dust) | Largest raw material's state, then 'Liquid' |
| Product families | SDS profile: Storage temperature min/max (°C) | S7 'Store between …' sentence; S10 'temperatures above … °C' condition | No storage-temperature sentence; generic S10 items |
| Product families | SDS profile: Water-based (Auto / Yes / No) | S7 'Protect from freezing.'; S10 'freezing' condition | Auto: water CAS 7732-18-5 at or above the threshold (default 20%) of the formula |
| Product families | SDS profile: Typical pH min/max | S9 pH '(typical)' | 'Not determined' (unless a per-product pH edit exists) |
| CAS determinations | Corrosive, acute-toxicity and aquatic categories; LD50/LC50; M-factors | S11 routes, symptoms, acute toxicity and ATEmix; S12 aquatic table; S13 D002/D003; S14 Class 8/6.1/9, marine pollutant and special-precautions fragments | Not classified. Default category ATEs are used silently; a missing M-factor prints '(M = 1, default)' |
| CAS determinations | STOT target organs and route (STOT Target Organs tab) | H370-H373 statements in S2 and the S4/S11 symptoms; S11 STOT RE line | Standard statement without organs; organs on only some CAS behind a code raise a preview warning |
| CAS determinations | Acrylate flag (CAS Descriptions; seed preview) | S10 acrylate inhibitor sentence on UV sheets; S4 acrylate names on UV sheets | Not determined: legacy acrylate CAS list (one release) or the name rule decides |
| CAS determinations | Element flags (nitrogen, sulfur, halogen) | S5 combustion products sentence; S10 Hazardous Decomposition Products | Carbon monoxide and carbon dioxide only |
| CAS determinations | Competent Person Determination for CAS with no federal hazard data | Missing-hazard-data publish gate | Publishing blocked for a no-data CAS at or above the threshold |
| CAS determinations | TC metals per CAS (cas_master.tc_metals) | S13 D004-D011 lines for metal compounds | Only direct RCRA list matches print |
| Regulatory lists | RCRA waste codes | S13 RCRA component lines; whether the UV cure-residue sentence can print | 'No RCRA hazardous waste characteristic…' sentence |
| Regulatory lists | SARA 313 list (de minimis, PBT and special-concern/PFAS flags) and TRI categories | S15 SARA 313 lines and range note; S12 PBT line | 'None' sentence |
| Regulatory lists | HAP list and 112(b) categories | S15 HAP rows and total | Only manual HAPs print |
| Regulatory lists | Carcinogen list | S11 carcinogenicity line and component blocks | 'None listed at or above 0.1%' sentence |
| Regulatory lists | Exempt VOC list | S9 Solids (wt%) | Only the raw's own exempt VOC % is used |
| Settings | sds.water_based_threshold_pct (Water-Based Threshold (%)) | Water-based rule on Auto (S7/S10 freezing wording) | 20% |
| Settings | sds.inhalation_only_cas | Carbon black/TiO2 rule; S3 row inclusion | Default 1333-86-4 and 13463-67-7 |
| Settings | sds.block_publish_missing / sds.missing_threshold_pct | Missing-hazard-data publish gate | Block on, threshold 1% (config default) |
| Settings | sds.physical_states / sds.color_options | Dropdowns for S9 Physical State and Color | Built-in defaults |

S13 and S14 never read it | Calculated value. A flash or boiling point edit that is not a temperature or contradicts the classification is not printed (preview warning) |
| SDS editor (per product) | Section 9 Appendix D overrides (odor threshold, pH, melting point, evaporation rate, flammability, limits, vapor pressure/density, partition coefficient, auto-ignition, decomposition, viscosity) | The matching S9 lines | 'Not determined' (family typical pH for pH; 'Not applicable' for flammability (solid, gas) on a Liquid) |
| SDS editor (per product) | Section 4 symptoms override | S4 and S11 Most Important Symptoms/Effects | Derived from the H3xx statements |
| SDS editor (per product) | Section 14 overrides (UN number, shipping name, class, PG, environmental hazards, special precautions) | S14 lines; class and environmental edits also steer the special-precautions fragments; a complete UN/PSN/class/PG set clears the transport publish block | Classifier output. A UN number or class override without all four core fields blocks publishing |
| Nothing to maintain | Transport in Bulk, Contaminated Packaging | S14 and S13 fixed sentences | Always printed |

## Publish gates (technical detail)

| Gate | Effect | Paths | Clears it |
|---|---|---|---|
| Company emergency phone blank (SDSReadinessService::companyEmergencyPhoneErrorFromDb :350) | blocks publishing | Manual FG publish (SDSController:533), resale publish (:937), SDS Updates republish (SDSUpdateController:305), bulk publish and cron (BulkPublishController:621, cron/bulk-publish.php:80; only private-label items run), alias publish incl. send queue and auto-send (AliasPublisher:177) | Enter an Emergency Phone in Admin > Settings |
| Private-label manufacturer emergency phone blank (manufacturerEmergencyPhoneError :361) | blocks publishing | PrivateLabelPublisher::publishOne :350, used by manual private-label publish/republish, SDS Updates "Republish Private Labels Only" and bulk/cron (publishItemFromBase) | Enter the manufacturer's emergency phone on the Manufacturers screen |
| Private-label manufacturer name blank (manufacturerNameError :492) | blocks publishing | PrivateLabelPublisher::publishOne :355 (every private-label path); also shown in the private-label preview Warnings box | Enter the manufacturer's Name on the Manufacturers screen |
| Form validation: blank Emergency Phone in Admin > Settings; blank Name or Emergency Phone on a manufacturer | blocks publishing | Admin > Settings save refused (AdminController:358-363); ManufacturerController::validateName / validateEmergencyPhone; Manufacturer::update throws | Supply the value |
| Inactive finished good (inactiveFinishedGoodError :454) | blocks publishing | SDSController::publish :520, SDS Updates (SDSUpdateController:321), AliasPublisher:172; bulk publish skips inactive products; shown in the FG preview Warnings box | Set the product's Status to Active (Finished Goods > Edit) |
| Supplier warnings: company name or phone never saved or blank; private-label manufacturer phone blank | warning only | FG preview (SDSController:85), resale preview (:147), SDS Review page (sds-review/index.php), private-label preview (createManufacturerVariant :987-995) | Save the Company Name and Phone in Admin > Settings, or the manufacturer's phone |
| Preview Warnings box: "Publishing blocked: the company emergency phone number is blank..." | warning only | FG preview (SDSController:79-82), resale preview (:143-146); the private-label preview shows the manufacturer's version (createManufacturerVariant :981-984) | Enter the emergency phone |
| Missing federal hazard data: a CAS at or above sds.missing_threshold_pct with no hazard data and no active competent-person determination | blocks publishing | Every publish path: single finished-good publish, resale raw-material publish, SDS Updates republish, private-label publisher, alias publisher and the Bulk Publish worker. Auto-send never publishes, so it is not gated. | Create an active determination for each listed CAS, refresh federal data, raise the threshold (0.01-100%), or untick sds.block_publish_missing on Admin Settings |
| Flash point warnings: flash point below 23 °C with no boiling point, override Flammable Liquids category versus the formula flash point, a contradicting Section 9 flash or boiling point edit dropped, or a raw material with a flammable ingredient and no flash point | warning only | Preview Warnings box only (HTML preview and PDF preview pages). Not shown in publish messages. | Enter flash points and boiling points on the raw materials, or enter the tested flash point as a Section 9 Flash Point edit that agrees with the hazard override |
| Physical state default: no physical state on the finished good, its product family or any raw material (the sheet assumes Liquid, which affects the Flammable Liquids class, the carbon black/TiO2 rule and the combustible-dust test) | warning only | Preview Warnings box only (HTML preview and PDF preview pages). Not shown in publish messages. | Set Physical State on the finished good, a default physical state on its product family, or Physical State on the raw materials |
| Family physical-state note: the product has no physical state of its own and its family default differs from the state of its largest raw material | warning only | Preview Warnings box only (HTML preview and PDF preview pages). | Set Physical State on the product where the family default is wrong for it, or correct the family default |
| STOT target organs incomplete: H370-H373 comes from a CAS with organs and also from a CAS without them, a hazard override or a trade-secret entry (standard statement prints) | warning only | Preview Warnings box only (HTML preview and PDF preview pages). Not shown in publish messages. | Enter organs for every listed CAS on CAS determinations > STOT Target Organs (override and trade-secret sources cannot carry organs) |
| Combustible dust not assessed: Powder/Solid product, no raw marked Yes, and a Powder/Solid raw still 'Not assessed' | warning only | Preview Warnings box and the SDS Creation Readiness page (finished goods and resale raw materials). Not shown in publish messages. | Set Combustible Dust to Yes or No on each listed raw material (Raw materials > Edit) |

| Company emergency phone blank | blocks publishing | Manual FG, resale and alias publish; on-demand alias publish for the send queue, auto-send and shipped-SDS export; SDS Update 'Republish Selected' (whole selection). Bulk/cron publishes private-label items only and marks the job failed. Not checked by private-label republish or 'Republish Private Labels Only'. | Save Admin > Settings > Emergency Phone. |
| Private-label manufacturer emergency phone or name blank | blocks publishing | Every private-label publish (PrivateLabelPublisher::publishOne): create/edit 'Publish now', Republish, Republish stale, 'Republish Private Labels Only', the cascade from FG publish and SDS Update, and bulk private-label items. | Enter the Emergency Phone and Name on the manufacturer record. |
| Missing federal hazard data at/above threshold | blocks publishing | All paths, checked on the first language: manual FG and resale, SDS Update, private-label republish and cascade, on-demand alias publish, and bulk (FG, resale and private-label items). Not shown in previews or on the readiness page. | Import federal data, add an active CAS determination, enter manual hazard data for no-CAS raws, or switch the gate off / raise the threshold. |
| Trade-secret constituent on the Prop 65 list | blocks publishing | All paths: manual FG and resale, SDS Update, private-label republish, on-demand alias publish, and bulk (FG, resale and private-label items). Shown in previews and on the readiness page. | Have the vendor disclose the chemical and clear the trade-secret box, or correct the CAS. |
| Section 14 transport 'Not determined' (unsupported class or 3+8+6.1 precedence) or incomplete override | blocks publishing | Every path, in every generated language: manual FG and resale, SDS Update, private-label republish, on-demand alias publish, and bulk (one failing language fails every item of the product). Shown in FG, resale and private-label previews; not on the readiness page. | Correct the raw-material H-codes / physical state, or enter UN Number, Proper Shipping Name, Hazard Class and Packing Group overrides in every language (resale: readiness page 'Edit SDS text'). A missing flash point never blocks, and a Special Precautions override has no effect on the gate. |
| Inactive finished good | blocks publishing | Manual FG publish, SDS Update, on-demand alias publish, private-label republish (group skipped), and bulk (not a candidate). Shown in the FG preview. | Set the product's Status to Active. |
| TSCA inventory not verified / not listed / INACTIVE entry | warning only | Flashed after manual FG and resale publish. Shown in every preview and on the readiness page. Silent on SDS Update, bulk/cron, private-label and alias publishes. | Inventory import, TSCA Review determinations (Listed with an NOA note for INACTIVE), and complete constituents with CAS. |
| n.o.s. shipping name without technical names | warning only | Previews only. | A Section 14 Proper Shipping Name override, or disclosed constituents with hazard data. |
| Flash point / boiling point checks (contradicting Section 9 edit, IBP assumed, override vs formula flash point, flammable ingredient in a raw with no flash point, non-temperature edit) | warning only | Previews only. | Enter flash points and boiling points on the raws, or correct or remove the Section 9 edit (keep it only for a measured value). |
| STOT target organs entered for only some sources of H370–H373 | warning only | Previews only. Sections 2, 4 and 11 print the generic statement. | Enter organs (and route) for every CAS behind the code on CAS Determinations > STOT Target Organs; a code that also comes from a hazard override or trade-secret declaration stays generic. |
| Combustible dust not assessed on a Powder/Solid product | warning only | Every preview (FG, resale, private label) and the readiness page, for finished goods and resale raws. | Set Combustible Dust (Yes or No) on each listed Powder/Solid raw material. |
| Incomplete constituent data | warning only | Previews and the readiness page. | Enter constituents with CAS and percentage on each listed raw. |
| Supplier block gaps (company name/phone never saved or blank; private-label manufacturer phone blank) and data gaps (missing SG/VOC, default 'Liquid' state, family default state replacing the largest raw's state) | warning only | FG and resale previews and the readiness page (company items); private-label preview (manufacturer phone); previews and the formula Calculate page (SG/VOC); previews only (physical-state notes). | Fill in the setting or record named in the warning (Physical State on the product, or the family default). |
| Bulk eligibility: unreviewed RM, freshness, sub-FG dependency, inactive FG, resale candidate rule | blocks publishing | Bulk/cron only. Ineligible products are left out of the job, not failed. | Review the RMs, edit or bump the product (Bump ALL after a code wording change), publish the intermediates, activate the FG, set up the alias. |
| Private-label structural checks (retired item, inactive FG, no base SDS, alias mismatch, manufacturer missing, missing language data, version race) | blocks publishing | Private-label republish, cascade and bulk private-label items. | Restore the item, publish the base, fix the alias or manufacturer, then retry. |
| Cron blackout / auto bulk publish off / runner lock | blocks publishing | Bulk publish (the toggle gates only the CMS-sync run; blackout and lock also apply to the admin Start button). | Wait for the window or the other runner to finish, or change the settings. |
| Permission checks | blocks publishing | All manual publish and edit actions (SDS, SDS Updates, Bulk Publish, Private Label, SDS Send Queue). Not the alias publishes that senders start on demand, or cron. | Give the role the edit permission. |
| Section 14 transport 'Not determined' or incomplete Section 14 override | blocks publishing | Every language on manual FG publish (SDSController :571), resale publish (:974), SDS Update republish (SDSUpdateController :362), alias publish (AliasPublisher :205), private-label publish (PrivateLabelPublisher :222) and the bulk/cron worker (publish-worker.php :168, :238). The preview shows the reason in Warnings (SDSGenerator :329, :751). | Correct the hazard data that produced explosive, gas, aerosol, flammable-solid, pyrophoric, water-reactive, oxidizer or peroxide codes (or a Gas state, including one set as a family default). Alternatively, enter UN number, proper shipping name, hazard class and packing group as Section 14 overrides on every language. A missing flash point does not trigger this gate; the sheet prints 'Not regulated'. A Special Precautions override does not affect it. |
| Trade-secret constituent on the Prop 65 list | blocks publishing | SDSController :547 (FG) and :949 (resale), SDSUpdateController :332, AliasPublisher :198, PrivateLabelPublisher :205, publish-worker.php :160, :210; preview warning (SDSGenerator :341, :763) | Have the vendor disclose the chemical and clear the trade-secret box on the raw material's constituent, or correct the CAS number |
| Missing federal hazard data for a CAS at or above the threshold | blocks publishing | First language on SDSController :561 and :964, SDSUpdateController :355, AliasPublisher :199, PrivateLabelPublisher :214, publish-worker.php :161, :227 | Create a Competent Person Determination for each listed CAS, or change sds.missing_threshold_pct or turn off sds.block_publish_missing (Admin > Settings) |
| n.o.s. shipping name without technical names | warning only | generate()/generateFromBase() add a preview warning (SDSGenerator :311-312, :733-734); never printed | Enter the Section 14 Proper Shipping Name override |
| Flash point / boiling point consistency warnings | warning only | SDSGenerator::flammabilityWarnings :4692 (with vetFlashPointEdit :4616, vetBoilingPointEdit :4658): a Section 9 edit dropped as contradictory, Category 2 assumed with no IBP, an FG override disagreeing with the printed flash point, or a raw with a flammable ingredient and no flash point | Enter flash and boiling points on the named raw materials, correct or reset the Section 9 edit, or enter the tested flash point as a Section 9 edit when an FG override sets the class |
| Raw-material data gaps (specific gravity, VOC, physical state default) | warning only | FormulaCalcService::dataGapWarnings :661 and SDSGenerator PHYSICAL_STATE_DEFAULT_WARNING :2996; preview and formula Calculate page only | Enter Specific Gravity, VOC wt% (or the <1% flag) and Physical State on the named raw materials or the finished good, or a default physical state on its family |
| Family default physical state overrides the largest raw material's state | warning only | SDSGenerator::familyPhysicalStateWarning :1243 via applyFamilyProfile :1229 (generate, computeBase, resale); preview only | Set Physical State on the product where the family default is wrong for it, or correct the family's default physical state |
| STOT target organs entered for only some CAS behind H370-H373 | warning only | StotTargetOrgans::warnings :249, added in SDSGenerator::generate :355-358 and generateFromBase :777-780; the sheet prints the standard statement | Enter organs (and route) for every listed CAS on CAS Determinations > STOT Target Organs, or clear them |
| Combustible dust not assessed on Powder/Solid raw materials of a Powder/Solid product | warning only | SDSReadinessService::combustibleDustNotAssessedWarning (SDS Creation Readiness, and SDSGenerator :362-366, :784-788 preview / publish flash); never printed, never blocks | Set Combustible Dust (Yes / No) on each named raw material (Raw Materials > Edit) from the vendor SDS Section 2 |
| Section 9 temperature edit that is not a temperature | warning only | SDSGenerator::temperatureOverrideWarnings :2109; the edit is neither printed nor classified from | Correct the edit to a number with °C or °F, or reset it to automatic in SDS > Edit Text |
| Substance sheet with no CAS constituent | warning only | SDSGenerator::generate :319-322 and generateFromBase :741-744 (section3 identity_missing) | Add the CAS constituent on the raw material, or set the product to Mixture |
